Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The factorial operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 929. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 376 with 1 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the factorial space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 929 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 376. QED.
